// Offsale / retired cards (still owned by players, can't be pulled). LEGACY order = rarest first.
const LEGACY = ['LEPRECHAUN', 'I LOVE YOU'];
const RETIRED = [
  ['LEPRECHAUN', 'god-tier', 75000, ['#7dff8a', '#00a83a'], 'lucky'],
  ['I LOVE YOU', 'god-tier', 15000, ['#ff9ab0', '#ff2d55'], 'love'],
  ['Rose', 'common', 5, ['#ff5c7a', '#b5173b']], ['Cupid', 'legendary', 250, ['#ffd1dc', '#ff6f91']],

  ['Pot of Gold', 'mythic', 700, ['#ffe066', '#d49a00']],

const CARDS = {};      // name -> card info
const PACK_ODDS = {};  // pack -> [{n, ch}]
(function buildCatalog() {
  for (const [pk, p] of Object.entries(PACKS)) {
    p.key = pk; p.count = p.count || 5;
    for (const [n, r, v, ch, col, fx, fixedOdds] of p.cards) {
      if (!CARDS[n]) CARDS[n] = { n, r, v, col, fx: fx || '', pack: pk, ch, fixedOdds: !!fixedOdds };
    }
    // "Easier" odds: god-tiers x2.5 and legendary/mythic weighted up, commons give the room back.
    // Packs marked fixed (and fixed cards like PIRATE TOKEN) keep their exact odds.
    let list = p.cards.map(c => ({ n: c[0], r: c[1], ch: c[3], fixed: !!c[6] }));
    if (!p.fixed) {
      list.forEach(c => { if (isGod(c.r)) c.ch *= 2.5; });
      const godSum = list.filter(c => isGod(c.r)).reduce((s, c) => s + c.ch, 0);
      const rest = list.filter(c => !isGod(c.r));
      rest.forEach(c => { c.w = c.ch * (c.r === 'common' ? 1 : 1.25); });
      const wSum = rest.reduce((s, c) => s + c.w, 0);
      rest.forEach(c => { c.ch = c.w / wSum * (100 - godSum); delete c.w; });
    } else {
      const fixedSum = list.filter(c => c.fixed).reduce((s, c) => s + c.ch, 0);
      const free = list.filter(c => !c.fixed), fSum = free.reduce((s, c) => s + c.ch, 0);
      free.forEach(c => { c.ch = c.ch / fSum * (100 - fixedSum); });
    }
    PACK_ODDS[pk] = list;
    list.forEach(c => { if (CARDS[c.n].pack === pk) CARDS[c.n].ch = c.ch; });
  }
  for (const [n, r, v, col, fx] of RETIRED) CARDS[n] = { n, r, v, col, fx: fx || '', pack: 'legacy', ch: 0, offsale: true };
})();
const cardInfo = n => CARDS[n];
const PACK_NAMES = { school: 'School', castle: 'Castle', cyber: 'Cyber', horror: 'Horror', space: 'Space', forest: 'Forest', ocean: 'Ocean',
  thankful: 'Thankful', food: 'Food', games: 'Games', golden: 'Golden', cave: 'Cave', legacy: 'Legacy' };
const fmtPct = ch => ch >= 1 ? ch.toFixed(ch >= 10 ? 1 : 2).replace(/\.?0+$/, '') + '%' :
  ch >= 0.01 ? ch.toFixed(3).replace(/0+$/, '') + '%' : '1 in ' + Math.round(100 / ch).toLocaleString('en-US');
function rarityOrder() {
  const all = Object.values(CARDS);
  return [...LEGACY.map(n => CARDS[n]), ...all.filter(c => !c.offsale).sort((a, b) => a.ch - b.ch || b.v - a.v),
    ...all.filter(c => c.offsale && !LEGACY.includes(c.n)).sort((a, b) => b.v - a.v)];
}

const fmt = n => Math.round(Number(n) || 0).toLocaleString('en-US');
const slug = n => n.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-');

// Special art backgrounds for some god-tiers.
const ART_BG = {
  gta: ['#ff7aa8', '#5a1c7a'], fortnite: ['#62c6ff', '#1d3b9e'], pirate: ['#3b2a00', '#0d0800'], moon: ['#1d0f33', '#070b24'],
  hacker: ['#03241f', '#000a08'], area51: ['#0a2a12', '#000'], fossil: ['#2a2040', '#050208'], lucky: ['#0a3a18', '#021208'], love: ['#4a0a22', '#14020a'],
};

const SPECIAL_DEFS = `<linearGradient id="gtaT" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#ff4fa3"/><stop offset=".55" stop-color="#ff8a5c"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#ffd36e"/></linearGradient>     <linearGradient id="gtaS" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#ffe27a"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#ff4f8b"/></linearGradient> <radialGradient id="ptG" cx=".38" cy=".32" r=".8"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#fff6c2"/><stop offset=".45" stop-color="#ffd23f"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#9c6a00"/></radialGradient> <radialGradient id="hmG" cx=".38" cy=".35" r=".75"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#fff2c4"/><stop offset=".55" stop-color="#ffb347"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#e8772e"/></radialGradient>`;
let artDefsDone = false;
function ensureArtDefs() {
  if (artDefsDone) return; artDefsDone = true;
  const defs = Object.values(CARDS).map(c => `<linearGradient id="g-${slug(c.n)}" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0.3" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="${c.col[0]}"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="${c.col[1]}"/></linearGradient>`).join('');
  const d = document.createElement('div');
  d.innerHTML = `<svg width="0" height="0" style="position:absolute" aria-hidden="true"><defs>${defs}${SPECIAL_DEFS}</defs></svg>`;
  document.body.prepend(d.firstChild);
}
function artSVG(c) {
  ensureArtDefs();
  const g = `url(#g-${slug(c.n)})`;
  const inner = CUSTOM[c.n] ? CUSTOM[c.n](g) : ico(ICON_ALIAS[c.n] || c.n, g);
  return `<svg class="tc-ico" viewBox="0 0 512 512" aria-hidden="true">${inner}</svg>`;
}

// opts: size 'xs'|'sm'|'md'|'lg', count, trait, isNew, valueTop, cls, attrs
function cardHTML(name, opts = {}) {
  const c = CARDS[name];
  if (!c) return '';
  const R = RAR[c.r];
  const bg = ART_BG[c.fx] || PACKS[c.pack]?.bg || ['#2a1030', '#0b0410'];
  const style = `--c1:${c.col[0]};--c2:${c.col[1]};--b1:${bg[0]};--b2:${bg[1]};--rc:${R.color};${opts.xstyle || ''}`;
  return `<div class="tc r-${c.r} ${c.fx ? 'fx fx-' + c.fx : ''} sz-${opts.size || 'md'} ${opts.trait === 'upside-down' ? 'upside' : ''} ${opts.valueTop ? 'vtop' : ''} ${opts.cls || ''}" style="${style}" ${opts.attrs || ''}>
  <div class="tc-in">
    ${opts.valueTop ? `<div class="tc-vbig">${fmt(c.v)}<small>c</small></div>` : ''}
    <div class="tc-head"><span class="tc-name">${esc(c.n)}</span></div>
    <div class="tc-art"><div class="tc-fx"></div>${artSVG(c)}<div class="tc-fx2"></div><div class="tc-sheen"></div></div>
    <div class="tc-foot"><span class="tc-gem"></span><span class="tc-rl">${R.label}</span>${opts.valueTop ? '' : `<span class="tc-v">${fmt(c.v)}</span>`}</div>
  </div>
  ${opts.count > 1 ? `<span class="tc-count">x${opts.count}</span>` : ''}${opts.isNew ? '<span class="tc-new">NEW</span>' : ''}${opts.trait === 'upside-down' ? '<span class="tc-glitch">GLITCHED</span>' : ''}${opts.locked ? '<span class="tc-lock">IN TRADE</span>' : ''}
</div>`;
}

const FB_CONFIG = {
  apiKey: 'AIzaSyDkw-xjR0eAkCPR3BRQyKk0Rnusu3pbEmw',

  databaseURL: 'https://cardio-game-3713-default-rtdb.firebaseio.com',
  projectId: 'cardio-game-3713',
};
const ROOT = 'saves/~legacy';
let db = null;
function initDB() {
  if (!window.firebase) throw new Error('no firebase');
  if (!firebase.apps.length) firebase.initializeApp(FB_CONFIG);
  db = firebase.database();
}
const dbRef = p => db.ref(ROOT + '/' + p);
const INC = n => firebase.database.ServerValue.increment(n);
const TS = () => firebase.database.ServerValue.TIMESTAMP;
const newId = () => Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8);

/* ---------- names + chat safety ---------- */
const nameKey = n => n.trim().toLowerCase();
const BAD = ['fuck', 'fuk', 'shit', 'bitch', 'asshole', 'dick', 'cock', 'pussy', 'cunt', 'bastard', 'slut', 'whore', 'nigg', 'nigga', 'fag', 'retard', 'porn', 'nude', 'sex', 'penis', 'vagina', 'boob', 'kys', 'killyourself', 'rape', 'damn', 'wtf', 'stfu'];
const leet = s => s.toLowerCase().replace(/0/g, 'o').replace(/1|!/g, 'i').replace(/3/g, 'e').replace(/4|@/g, 'a').replace(/5|\$/g, 's').replace(/7/g, 't');
function hasBad(s) { const t = leet(s).replace(/[^a-z]/g, ''); return BAD.some(w => t.includes(w)); }
function nameProblem(n) {
  n = n.trim();
  if (n.length < 3) return 'Names need at least 3 letters.';
  if (n.length > 16) return 'Names can be 16 letters max.';
  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_]+$/.test(n)) return 'Use only letters, numbers and _ (no spaces).';
  if (hasBad(n)) return 'Pick a different name.';
  return '';
}
function cleanChat(s) {
  s = String(s).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 140);
  s = s.replace(/(https?:\/\/|www\.)\S+/gi, '[link removed]').replace(/\b\S+\.(com|net|org|gg|io|me|tv|xyz|co)\b\S*/gi, '[link removed]');
  s = s.replace(/\S+@\S+\.\S+/g, '[removed]');
  s = s.replace(/(\d[\s\-().]*){7,}/g, '[number removed]');
  s = s.split(' ').map(w => hasBad(w) ? '*'.repeat(Math.min(w.length, 6)) : w).join(' ');
  return s;
}

/* ================= GAME ================= */
const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
const $$ = s => [...document.querySelectorAll(s)];
const sleep = ms => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));
const withTimeout = (p, ms) => Promise.race([p, new Promise((_, rej) => setTimeout(() => rej(new Error('timeout')), ms))]);
const mySid = newId();
let S = { key: null, u: null, kicked: false };
let qty = 1, lastPack = null, curTab = 'shop', invFilter = 'all', rankMode = 'value';
const locked = new Set();              // card ids that are in a live trade offer
let pendingCreate = null, revealTimer = null, renderTimer = null, boardTimer = null, lastCoins = null;

function toast(msg, kind = '') {
  const t = document.createElement('div');
  t.className = 'toast ' + kind; t.textContent = msg;
  $('#toasts').appendChild(t);
  setTimeout(() => t.remove(), 3400);
  while ($('#toasts').children.length > 4) $('#toasts').firstChild.remove();
}
function modal(html, cls = '', sticky = false) {
  const root = $('#modal-root');
  root.innerHTML = `<div class="modal-bg"><div class="modal ${cls}">${sticky ? '' : '<button class="x" data-close>&times;</button>'}${html}</div></div>`;
  const bg = root.firstChild;
  if (!sticky) bg.addEventListener('click', e => { if (e.target === bg || e.target.closest('[data-close]')) closeModal(); });
  return bg.querySelector('.modal');
}
function closeModal() { $('#modal-root').innerHTML = ''; }
function askConfirm(text, ok = 'Yes', kind = 'pri') {
  return new Promise(res => {
    const m = modal(`<h2 class="h" style="font-size:22px;margin-bottom:10px;">Are you sure?</h2><p style="font-size:15px;line-height:1.45;">${text}</p>
      <div class="cd-actions"><button class="btn ${kind}" data-ok>${ok}</button><button class="btn" data-close>Cancel</button></div>`);
    m.style.maxWidth = '420px';
    m.querySelector('[data-ok]').onclick = () => { closeModal(); res(true); };
    m.querySelectorAll('[data-close]').forEach(b => b.addEventListener('click', () => res(false)));
  });
}

/* ---------- inventory helpers ---------- */
function invList() {
  const inv = (S.u && S.u.inv) || {};
  return Object.entries(inv).filter(([, c]) => c && CARDS[c.n]).map(([id, c]) => ({ id, n: c.n, t: c.t || '', at: c.at || 0 }));
}
function stacks(list) {
  const m = new Map();
  for (const c of list) {
    const k = c.n + '|' + c.t;
    if (!m.has(k)) m.set(k, { n: c.n, t: c.t, ids: [], at: 0 });
    const s = m.get(k); s.ids.push(c.id); s.at = Math.max(s.at, c.at);
  }
  return [...m.values()];
}
const worthOf = list => list.reduce((s, c) => s + CARDS[c.n].v, 0);
const betterCard = (a, b) => !b || RAR[CARDS[a].r].rank > RAR[CARDS[b].r].rank || (RAR[CARDS[a].r].rank === RAR[CARDS[b].r].rank && CARDS[a].v > CARDS[b].v);
const canAct = () => S.u && !S.kicked;
function writeUser(upd) {
  return dbRef('users/' + S.key).update(upd).catch(e => { toast('Could not save: ' + (e.message || e)); throw e; });
}
// optimistic local copy so spam-opening never waits on the network
function applyLocal(upd) {
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(upd)) {
    const parts = k.split('/'); let o = S.u;
    for (const p of parts.slice(0, -1)) { o[p] = o[p] || {}; o = o[p]; }
    const last = parts[parts.length - 1];
    if (v && typeof v === 'object' && v['.sv'] && v['.sv'].increment !== undefined) o[last] = (o[last] || 0) + v['.sv'].increment;
    else if (v === null) delete o[last]; else o[last] = v;
  }
  onUserChanged();
}
function incVal(v) { return v && v['.sv'] && v['.sv'].increment; }

/* ---------- LOGIN ---------- */
function loginMsg(t) { $('#login-msg').textContent = t; }
function renderLoginFan() {
  const show = ['THE HACKER', 'KRAKEN', 'GTA VI', 'CORNUCOPIA', 'PIRATE TOKEN'];
  $('#login-fan').innerHTML = show.map((n, i) => {
    const a = (i - 2) * 11, x = (i - 2) * 92, y = Math.abs(i - 2) * 16;
    return cardHTML(n, { size: 'sm', xstyle: `transform:translateX(calc(-50% + ${x}px)) translateY(${y}px) rotate(${a}deg);z-index:${10 - Math.abs(i - 2)}` });
  }).join('');
}
async function doLogin() {
  const raw = $('#login-name').value.trim();
  const prob = nameProblem(raw);
  if (prob) return loginMsg(prob);
  try { if (!db) initDB(); } catch (e) { return loginMsg("Can't reach the game server. Check your internet and try again."); }
  const k = nameKey(raw);
  loginMsg(''); $('#login-go').disabled = true; $('#login-go').textContent = 'Connecting...';
  try {
    const snap = await withTimeout(dbRef('users/' + k).once('value'), 10000);
    const u = snap.val();
    if (!u) {
      if (pendingCreate !== k) {
        pendingCreate = k;
        loginMsg(`No player named ${raw} yet. Tap again to create this account.`);
        $('#login-go').disabled = false; $('#login-go').textContent = 'Create ' + raw; return;
      }
      await dbRef('users/' + k).set({ name: raw, coins: 500, created: TS(), sid: mySid, stats: { packs: 0 } });
    } else {
      await dbRef('users/' + k + '/sid').set(mySid);
    }
    try { localStorage.setItem('cardio-last', raw); } catch (e) {}
    enterGame(k);
  } catch (e) {
    loginMsg("Can't reach the game server right now. Try again in a moment.");
    $('#login-go').disabled = false; $('#login-go').textContent = 'Play';
  }
}

function enterGame(k) {
  S.key = k;
  $('#login').classList.add('hidden');
  $('#game').classList.remove('hidden');
  let first = true;
  dbRef('users/' + k).on('value', snap => {
    const u = snap.val();
    if (!u) return;
    u.inv = u.inv || {}; u.stats = u.stats || {}; u.dex = u.dex || {};
    S.u = u;
    onUserChanged();
    if (first) { first = false; afterFirstLoad(); }
  });
  dbRef('users/' + k + '/sid').on('value', snap => {
    const v = snap.val();
    if (v && v !== mySid && !S.kicked) kicked();
  });
  const pres = dbRef('online/' + k);
  db.ref('.info/connected').on('value', s => {
    if (s.val() === false) return;
    pres.onDisconnect().remove();
    pres.set({ n: S.u ? S.u.name : $('#login-name').value.trim(), at: TS() });
  });
  Trade.start(k);
  startAuction();
}
async function afterFirstLoad() {
  $('#me-name').textContent = S.u.name;
  $('#me-av').textContent = S.u.name[0].toUpperCase();
  renderPacks(); showTab('shop');
  // auction coins held when the page was closed come back
  const esc_ = S.u.escrow;
  if (esc_ && typeof esc_ === 'object') {
    const back = Object.values(esc_).reduce((s, v) => s + (Number(v) || 0), 0);
    if (back > 0) {
      const upd = { coins: INC(back), escrow: null };
      applyLocal(upd); writeUser(upd);
      toast(`${fmt(back)} coins came back from an auction you left.`, 'gold');
    }
  }
  if (!S.u.legacyUsed) legacyModal(true); // shows every login until the one-time import is used
}
function onUserChanged() {
  if (!S.u) return;
  const c = Math.max(0, Math.round(S.u.coins || 0));
  $('#coin-n').textContent = fmt(c);
  if (lastCoins !== null && c !== lastCoins) { const el = $('#coins'); el.classList.remove('bump'); void el.offsetWidth; el.classList.add('bump'); }
  lastCoins = c;
  clearTimeout(renderTimer);
  renderTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    if (curTab === 'inv') renderInv();
    if (curTab === 'profile') renderProfile();
    if (curTab === 'shop') updatePackAfford();
    if (curTab === 'trade') Trade.render();
    updateStageAgain();
  }, 60);
  clearTimeout(boardTimer);
  boardTimer = setTimeout(updateBoard, 2500);
}
function updateBoard() {
  if (!canAct()) return;
  const list = invList();
  let best = null; list.forEach(c => { if (betterCard(c.n, best)) best = c.n; });
  dbRef('board/' + S.key).set({ n: S.u.name, w: worthOf(list), c: list.length, b: best, p: S.u.stats.packs || 0, at: TS() }).catch(() => {});
}
function kicked() {
  S.kicked = true;
  Trade.leaveAll && Trade.leaveAll();
  const m = modal(`<h2 class="h" style="font-size:24px;margin-bottom:8px;">Playing somewhere else</h2>
    <p style="line-height:1.5">Your account <b>${esc(S.u ? S.u.name : '')}</b> just logged in on another device or tab, so this one stopped to keep your cards safe.</p>
    <div class="cd-actions"><button class="btn pri" id="kick-back">Play here instead</button></div>`, '', true);
  $('#kick-back').onclick = async () => { await dbRef('users/' + S.key + '/sid').set(mySid); S.kicked = false; closeModal(); toast('Back in.', 'good'); };
}

/* ---------- TABS ---------- */
function showTab(t) {
  curTab = t;
  $$('.tabc').forEach(e => e.classList.add('hidden'));
  $('#tab-' + t).classList.remove('hidden');
  $$('.tab').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('on', b.dataset.tab === t));
  if (t === 'shop') updatePackAfford();
  if (t === 'inv') renderInv();
  if (t === 'rank') renderRank();
  if (t === 'odds') renderOdds();
  if (t === 'profile') renderProfile();
  if (t === 'auction') renderAuction();
  if (t === 'trade') Trade.render();
}

/* ---------- SHOP ---------- */
function isGlitch() {
  const d = new Date();
  return d.getDay() === 3 && d.getHours() === 16 && d.getMinutes() < 30;
}
function renderPacks() {
  $('#packs').innerHTML = Object.values(PACKS).map(p => {
    const odds = PACK_ODDS[p.key];
    const gods = odds.filter(c => isGod(CARDS[c.n].r));
    const chase = p.art ? { n: p.art } : [...odds].sort((a, b) => a.ch - b.ch)[0];
    const chaseTxt = gods.length > 1 ? `<b>${gods.length} chase cards</b><br>${gods.slice(0, 3).map(c => esc(c.n)).join(', ')}` : `Chase card<br><b>${esc(chase.n)}</b>`;
    return `<div class="pack" data-pack="${p.key}" style="--pc:${p.c}">
      ${p.isNew ? '<span class="p-tag">NEW</span>' : ''}
      <div class="p-art">${artSVG(CARDS[chase.n])}</div>
      <div class="p-name">${PACK_NAMES[p.key]}</div>
      <div class="p-chase">${chaseTxt}</div>
      <span class="p-cost"><span class="coin-ico"></span>${fmt(p.cost)}</span>
      <div class="p-count">${p.count} cards</div></div>`;
  }).join('');
  updatePackAfford();
}
function updatePackAfford() {
  if (!S.u) return;
  $$('.pack').forEach(el => el.classList.toggle('cant', S.u.coins < PACKS[el.dataset.pack].cost * qty));
}
function roll(pk) {
  const list = PACK_ODDS[pk];
  const r = Math.random() * 100; let acc = 0;
  for (const c of list) { acc += c.ch; if (r < acc) return c.n; }
  return list[0].n;
}
function openPack(pk, q = qty) {
  if (!canAct()) return;
  const p = PACKS[pk], cost = p.cost * q;
  if ((S.u.coins || 0) < cost) { toast(`Not enough coins. ${q > 1 ? 'x' + q + ' ' : ''}${PACK_NAMES[pk]} costs ${fmt(cost)}.`); return false; }
  const glitch = isGlitch();
  const pulls = [], upd = { coins: INC(-cost), 'stats/packs': INC(q) };
  const seen = new Set();
  let best = S.u.stats.best || null;
  for (let i = 0; i < q * p.count; i++) {
    const n = roll(pk), id = newId() + i;
    const card = { n, at: Date.now() };
    if (glitch && Math.random() < 0.25) card.t = 'upside-down';
    upd['inv/' + id] = card;
    pulls.push({ id, ...card, isNew: !S.u.dex[n] && !seen.has(n) });
    seen.add(n); upd['dex/' + n] = 1;
    if (betterCard(n, best)) { best = n; upd['stats/best'] = n; }
  }
  applyLocal(upd); writeUser(upd);
  const tile = $(`.pack[data-pack="${pk}"]`);
  if (tile) { tile.classList.remove('rip'); void tile.offsetWidth; tile.classList.add('rip'); }
  showStage(pk, q, pulls);
  return true;
}
function showStage(pk, q, pulls) {
  lastPack = pk; clearTimeout(revealTimer);
  const rank = c => RAR[CARDS[c.n].r].rank * 1e7 + CARDS[c.n].v;
  let order;
  if (q === 1) { order = [...pulls].sort((a, b) => rank(a) - rank(b)); } // best card flips last
  else order = [...pulls].sort((a, b) => rank(b) - rank(a));
  $('#stage').classList.remove('hidden');
  const box = $('#stage-cards'), W = Math.max(260, box.clientWidth - 24), H = Math.max(220, box.clientHeight - 30);
  let fs = 16;
  for (; fs > 4.5; fs -= 0.25) { const cols = Math.max(1, Math.floor(W / (fs * 16.2))); if (Math.ceil(order.length / cols) * fs * 22.2 <= H) break; }
  const size = 'md', xstyle = `--fs:${fs.toFixed(2)}px`;
  const step = q === 1 ? 140 : q === 5 ? 30 : 16;
  $('#stage-title').textContent = `${PACK_NAMES[pk]} Pack${q > 1 ? ' x' + q : ''}`;
  $('#stage-cards').innerHTML = order.map((c, i) => {
    const info = CARDS[c.n], R = RAR[info.r];
    return `<div class="pull ${R.rank >= 1 ? 'hi' : ''}" style="animation-delay:${i * step}ms;--glow:${R.rank >= 3 ? info.col[0] : R.color}">${cardHTML(c.n, { size, xstyle, trait: c.t, isNew: c.isNew, attrs: `data-n="${esc(c.n)}"` })}</div>`;
  }).join('');
  const counts = {};
  pulls.forEach(c => { const r = CARDS[c.n].r; counts[r] = (counts[r] || 0) + 1; });
  $('#stage-sum').innerHTML = Object.keys(RAR).filter(r => counts[r] && r !== 'common').reverse()
    .map(r => `<span class="chip on" style="--cc:${RAR[r].color}"><i></i>${counts[r]} ${RAR[r].label}</span>`).join('') +
    `<span class="chip on" style="--cc:var(--gold)">Worth ${fmt(worthOf(pulls))}c</span>`;
  $('#stage').classList.remove('hidden');
  updateStageAgain();
  const gods = pulls.filter(c => isGod(CARDS[c.n].r));
  if (gods.length) {
    const top = gods.sort((a, b) => rank(b) - rank(a))[0];
    const fl = $('#flash');
    fl.style.background = `radial-gradient(circle, ${CARDS[top.n].col[0]}, transparent 70%)`;
    const wait = q === 1 ? order.length * step + 250 : 300;
    revealTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      fl.classList.remove('go'); void fl.offsetWidth; fl.classList.add('go');
      const st = $('#stage'); st.classList.remove('shake'); void st.offsetWidth; st.classList.add('shake');
      revealTimer = setTimeout(() => showReveal(top.n, top.t), 550);
    }, wait);
  }
}
function updateStageAgain() {
  if (!lastPack || !S.u) return;
  const cost = PACKS[lastPack].cost * qty;
  const b = $('#stage-again');
  b.innerHTML = `Open Again <span style="opacity:.8;font-weight:800;margin-left:6px;">${fmt(cost)}c</span>`;
  b.disabled = (S.u.coins || 0) < cost;
}
function showReveal(n, t) {
  const c = CARDS[n], R = RAR[c.r];
  const el = $('#reveal');
  el.style.setProperty('--rc', c.col[0]);
  el.innerHTML = `<div class="rays"></div><div class="rv-r">${R.label} pull</div><h1>${esc(n)}</h1>
    <div class="rv-card">${cardHTML(n, { size: innerWidth > 600 ? 'xl' : 'lg', trait: t })}</div>
    <div class="rv-odds">${c.offsale ? 'Offsale' : 'Pull chance: ' + fmtPct(c.ch)} &middot; Worth ${fmt(c.v)} coins</div><div class="rv-tap">Tap to continue</div>`;
  el.classList.remove('hidden');
}
function hideReveal() { $('#reveal').classList.add('hidden'); $('#reveal').innerHTML = ''; }

/* ---------- COLLECTION ---------- */
const PULLABLE = Object.values(CARDS).filter(c => !c.offsale);
function renderInv() {
  if (!S.u) return;
  const list = invList();
  const dexOwned = PULLABLE.filter(c => S.u.dex[c.n]).length;
  let best = null; list.forEach(c => { if (betterCard(c.n, best)) best = c.n; });
  $('#inv-stats').innerHTML = `
    <div class="stat"><div class="k">Cards</div><div class="v">${fmt(list.length)}</div></div>
    <div class="stat"><div class="k">Collection worth</div><div class="v" style="color:var(--gold)">${fmt(worthOf(list))}c</div></div>
    <div class="stat"><div class="k">Card book</div><div class="v">${dexOwned}<span class="muted" style="font-size:15px"> / ${PULLABLE.length}</span></div><div class="bar"><i style="width:${dexOwned / PULLABLE.length * 100}%"></i></div></div>
    <div class="stat"><div class="k">Best card</div><div class="v" style="font-size:16px;color:${best ? RAR[CARDS[best].r].color : 'inherit'}">${best ? esc(best) : '-'}</div></div>`;
  const chips = [['all', 'All', '#fff'], ['common', 'Common', RAR.common.color], ['legendary', 'Legendary', RAR.legendary.color], ['mythic', 'Mythic', RAR.mythic.color], ['god', 'God-Tier+', RAR['god-tier'].color], ['glitch', 'Glitched', '#0ff']];
  $('#inv-chips').innerHTML = chips.map(([k, l, c]) => `<span class="chip ${invFilter === k ? 'on' : ''}" data-f="${k}" style="--cc:${c}"><i></i>${l}</span>`).join('');
  const q = $('#inv-q').value.trim().toLowerCase();
  let st = stacks(list).filter(s => {
    const r = CARDS[s.n].r;
    if (q && !s.n.toLowerCase().includes(q)) return false;
    if (invFilter === 'god') return isGod(r);
    if (invFilter === 'glitch') return !!s.t;
    if (invFilter !== 'all') return r === invFilter;
    return true;
  });
  const sort = $('#inv-sort').value;
  const rk = s => RAR[CARDS[s.n].r].rank;
  st.sort(sort === 'rarity' ? (a, b) => rk(b) - rk(a) || CARDS[b.n].v - CARDS[a.n].v :
    sort === 'new' ? (a, b) => b.at - a.at : sort === 'name' ? (a, b) => a.n.localeCompare(b.n) :
    sort === 'count' ? (a, b) => b.ids.length - a.ids.length : (a, b) => CARDS[b.n].v - CARDS[a.n].v);
  $('#inv-grid').innerHTML = st.length ? st.map(s => cardHTML(s.n, { size: 'sm', count: s.ids.length, trait: s.t, locked: s.ids.every(id => locked.has(id)), attrs: `data-st="${esc(s.n)}|${s.t}"` })).join('')
    : `<div class="empty"><b>${list.length ? 'No cards match that.' : 'No cards yet.'}</b>${list.length ? 'Try a different search or filter.' : 'Head to the Shop and rip some packs.'}</div>`;
}
function cardModal(n, t = '', fromInv = false) {
  const c = CARDS[n]; if (!c) return;
  const all = Object.values(CARDS);
  const moneyRank = [...all].sort((a, b) => b.v - a.v).findIndex(x => x.n === n) + 1;
  const rarRank = rarityOrder().findIndex(x => x.n === n) + 1;
  const mine = invList().filter(x => x.n === n && (!fromInv || x.t === t));
  const sellable = mine.filter(x => !locked.has(x.id));
  const m = modal(`<div class="cd">${cardHTML(n, { size: 'lg', trait: fromInv ? t : '' })}
    <div class="cd-info"><div class="rtag" style="--rc:${RAR[c.r].color};display:inline-block;margin-bottom:8px;">${RAR[c.r].label}</div>
      <h2>${esc(n)}</h2>
      <div class="kv"><span>Value</span><b style="color:var(--gold)">${fmt(c.v)} coins</b></div>
      <div class="kv"><span>Pack</span><b>${PACK_NAMES[c.pack]}</b></div>
      <div class="kv"><span>Pull chance</span><b class="${c.offsale ? 'offsale' : ''}">${c.offsale ? 'OFFSALE' : fmtPct(c.ch)}</b></div>
      <div class="kv"><span>Money rank</span><b>#${moneyRank}</b></div>
      <div class="kv"><span>Rarity rank</span><b>#${rarRank}</b></div>
      <div class="kv"><span>You own</span><b>${mine.length}</b></div>
      ${sellable.length ? `<div class="cd-actions"><button class="btn gold" data-sell="1">Sell 1 &middot; ${fmt(c.v)}c</button>${sellable.length > 1 ? `<button class="btn" data-sell="all">Sell all ${sellable.length} &middot; ${fmt(c.v * sellable.length)}c</button>` : ''}</div>` : ''}
    </div></div>`);
  m.querySelectorAll('[data-sell]').forEach(b => b.onclick = async () => {
    const ids = b.dataset.sell === 'all' ? sellable.map(x => x.id) : [sellable[0].id];
    if (isGod(c.r) && !(await askConfirm(`Sell ${ids.length > 1 ? ids.length + ' copies of ' : ''}<b>${esc(n)}</b> for ${fmt(c.v * ids.length)} coins? You can't undo this.`, 'Sell'))) return;
    sellIds(ids); closeModal();
  });
}
function sellIds(ids) {
  if (!canAct()) return;
  ids = ids.filter(id => !locked.has(id) && S.u.inv[id]);
  if (!ids.length) return;
  const v = ids.reduce((s, id) => s + CARDS[S.u.inv[id].n].v, 0);
  const upd = { coins: INC(v) };
  ids.forEach(id => { upd['inv/' + id] = null; });
  applyLocal(upd); writeUser(upd);
  toast(`Sold ${ids.length} card${ids.length > 1 ? 's' : ''} for ${fmt(v)} coins`, 'gold');
}
async function bulkSell(kind) {
  const list = invList().filter(c => !locked.has(c.id));
  let ids;
  if (kind === 'dupes') { const seen = new Set(); ids = []; [...list].sort((a, b) => a.at - b.at).forEach(c => { const k = c.n + '|' + c.t; if (seen.has(k)) ids.push(c.id); else seen.add(k); }); }
  else ids = list.filter(c => CARDS[c.n].r === kind).map(c => c.id);
  if (!ids.length) return toast('Nothing to sell there.');
  const v = ids.reduce((s, id) => s + CARDS[S.u.inv[id].n].v, 0);
  if (await askConfirm(`Sell <b>${ids.length}</b> card${ids.length > 1 ? 's' : ''} for <b>${fmt(v)}</b> coins?`, 'Sell them')) sellIds(ids);
}

/* ---------- RANKINGS ---------- */
const thumbHTML = n => { const c = CARDS[n], bg = ART_BG[c.fx] || PACKS[c.pack]?.bg || ['#2a1030', '#0b0410']; return `<div class="thumb" style="--b1:${bg[0]};--b2:${bg[1]};--rc:${RAR[c.r].color}">${artSVG(c)}</div>`; };
async function renderRank() {
  const modes = [['value', 'Most Valuable'], ['rare', 'Rarest'], ['players', 'Top Players']];
  $('#rank-chips').innerHTML = modes.map(([k, l]) => `<span class="chip ${rankMode === k ? 'on' : ''}" data-rk="${k}">${l}</span>`).join('');
  const body = $('#rank-body');
  if (rankMode === 'players') {
    $('#rank-sub').textContent = 'Every player, ranked by how much their whole collection is worth.';
    body.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">Loading players...</div>';
    let rows = [];
    try { const s = await withTimeout(dbRef('board').once('value'), 9000); rows = Object.entries(s.val() || {}).map(([k, v]) => ({ k, ...v })).filter(r => r.n); } catch (e) { body.innerHTML = '<div class="empty"><b>Could not load players.</b>Check your internet.</div>'; return; }
    if (rankMode !== 'players') return;
    rows.sort((a, b) => (b.w || 0) - (a.w || 0));
    const pod = [1, 0, 2].map(i => rows[i] ? `<div class="pod p${i + 1}">${rows[i].b && CARDS[rows[i].b] ? cardHTML(rows[i].b, { size: i === 0 ? 'md' : 'sm' }) : ''}<div class="stand"><div class="place">#${i + 1}</div><b>${esc(rows[i].n)}</b><div class="sub">${fmt(rows[i].w)}c</div></div></div>` : '').join('');
    body.innerHTML = `<div class="podium">${pod}</div><div class="rank-list">${rows.slice(3, 100).map((r, i) => `
      <div class="rrow ${r.k === S.key ? 'me' : ''}"><div class="rnum">${i + 4}</div><div class="thumb" style="--b1:#2a1030;--b2:#0b0410;--rc:var(--brand);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-weight:900;font-size:20px;">${esc(r.n[0].toUpperCase())}</div>
      <div class="rname">${esc(r.n)}<small>${fmt(r.c || 0)} cards${r.b ? ' &middot; best: ' + esc(r.b) : ''}</small></div><span class="rtag" style="--rc:var(--gold)">${fmt(r.p || 0)} packs</span><div class="rval">${fmt(r.w)}c<small>worth</small></div></div>`).join('')}</div>`;
    return;
  }
  $('#rank-sub').textContent = rankMode === 'value' ? 'Every card in the game, ranked by sell value.' : 'Every card in the game, ranked by how hard it is to pull. Offsale cards rank first.';
  const list = rankMode === 'value' ? Object.values(CARDS).sort((a, b) => b.v - a.v) : rarityOrder();
  const sub = c => c.offsale ? '<span class="offsale">OFFSALE</span>' : fmtPct(c.ch);
  const pod = [1, 0, 2].map(i => `<div class="pod p${i + 1}">${cardHTML(list[i].n, { size: i === 0 ? 'md' : 'sm', attrs: `data-cn="${esc(list[i].n)}"` })}<div class="stand"><div class="place">#${i + 1}</div><div class="sub">${rankMode === 'value' ? fmt(list[i].v) + 'c' : sub(list[i])}</div></div></div>`).join('');
  body.innerHTML = `<div class="podium">${pod}</div><div class="rank-list">${list.slice(3).map((c, i) => `
    <div class="rrow" data-cn="${esc(c.n)}"><div class="rnum">${i + 4}</div>${thumbHTML(c.n)}
    <div class="rname">${esc(c.n)}<small>${PACK_NAMES[c.pack]} pack</small></div>
    <span class="rtag" style="--rc:${RAR[c.r].color}">${RAR[c.r].label}</span>
    <div class="rval">${fmt(c.v)}c<small>${sub(c)}</small></div></div>`).join('')}</div>`;
}

/* ---------- ODDS ---------- */
function renderOdds() {
  const row = (n, right) => { const c = CARDS[n]; return `<div class="orow ${isGod(c.r) ? 'god' : ''}" style="--rc:${RAR[c.r].color};--c1:${c.col[0]}"><i></i><span>${esc(n)}</span><span>${right}</span></div>`; };
  const legacy = `<div class="odds-box" style="--pc:var(--brand)"><h4>Legacy <small class="offsale">OFFSALE</small></h4>${LEGACY.map((n, i) => row(n, `<span class="offsale">#${i + 1} rarest</span>`)).join('')}
    <div class="muted" style="font-size:12px;margin-top:8px;">These can't be pulled anymore. If you have one, it's yours forever.</div></div>`;
  $('#odds').innerHTML = legacy + Object.values(PACKS).map(p => `<div class="odds-box" style="--pc:${p.c}"><h4>${PACK_NAMES[p.key]}<small>${fmt(p.cost)}c &middot; ${p.count} cards</small></h4>
    ${[...PACK_ODDS[p.key]].sort((a, b) => b.ch - a.ch).map(c => row(c.n, fmtPct(c.ch))).join('')}</div>`).join('');
}

/* ---------- PROFILE ---------- */
function renderProfile() {
  if (!S.u) return;
  const u = S.u, list = invList();
  const joined = u.created ? new Date(u.created).toLocaleDateString() : '';
  $('#profile').innerHTML = `<div class="sec-head"><div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;"><span class="avatar" style="width:56px;height:56px;font-size:26px;">${esc(u.name[0].toUpperCase())}</span>
    <div><h2 class="h">${esc(u.name)}</h2><p>${joined ? 'Playing since ' + joined : ''}</p></div></div><button class="btn" id="logout">Log out</button></div>
    <div class="stats">
      <div class="stat"><div class="k">Coins</div><div class="v" style="color:var(--gold)">${fmt(u.coins)}</div></div>
      <div class="stat"><div class="k">Collection worth</div><div class="v">${fmt(worthOf(list))}c</div></div>
      <div class="stat"><div class="k">Packs opened</div><div class="v">${fmt(u.stats.packs || 0)}</div></div>
      <div class="stat"><div class="k">Best pull ever</div><div class="v" style="font-size:16px;${u.stats.best && CARDS[u.stats.best] ? 'color:' + RAR[CARDS[u.stats.best].r].color : ''}">${u.stats.best ? esc(u.stats.best) : '-'}</div></div>
    </div>
    <div class="notice" style="margin-bottom:14px;"><div><b>Your save is online.</b> Everything saves to the name <b>${esc(u.name)}</b> by itself. To play on another phone or computer, just type your name there. Only one device can play at a time.</div></div>
    ${u.legacyUsed ? '<div class="panel muted" style="font-size:13.5px;">You already used your one-time old save import.</div>' : `<div class="panel legacy-box"><h3 class="h" style="font-size:18px;">Bring in an old save code</h3>
      <p class="muted" style="margin:6px 0 0;font-size:13.5px;">Old save codes (from the old Data tab) work <b style="color:var(--gold)">one last time</b> because we switched to online saves.</p>
      <textarea class="field" id="legacy-txt2" placeholder="Paste your old save code here"></textarea><button class="btn gold" id="legacy-go2">Import my old save</button></div>`}`;
  $('#logout').onclick = () => { try { localStorage.removeItem('cardio-last'); } catch (e) {} dbRef('online/' + S.key).remove().finally(() => location.reload()); };
  const g = $('#legacy-go2');
  if (g) g.onclick = () => importLegacy($('#legacy-txt2').value);
}

/* ---------- OLD SAVE (one last time) ---------- */
function legacyModal(onLogin) {
  let pre = '';
  try { pre = localStorage.getItem('cardio-save-' + S.key) || ''; } catch (e) {}
  const m = modal(`<div class="legacy-box"><h2 class="h" style="font-size:24px;">We switched to online saves</h2>
    <p style="line-height:1.5;margin:10px 0 0;">Your cards now save to your name <b>${esc(S.u.name)}</b> automatically, on any device.</p>
    <div class="notice" style="margin-top:12px;"><div>Old save codes from the Data tab will work <b>ONE LAST TIME</b>. Paste yours below to bring your coins and cards over. After that, old codes stop working for good.</div></div>
    <textarea class="field" id="legacy-txt" placeholder="Paste your old save code here">${esc(pre)}</textarea>
    <div class="cd-actions"><button class="btn gold" id="legacy-go">Import my old save</button><button class="btn" id="legacy-skip">Not now</button></div>
    <p class="muted" style="font-size:12px;margin:10px 0 0;">This pops up every time you log in until you use it. It's also in the Profile tab. Card values come from the game, not the code.</p></div>`);
  $('#legacy-go').onclick = () => importLegacy($('#legacy-txt').value);
  $('#legacy-skip').onclick = () => closeModal();
}
async function importLegacy(text) {
  if (!canAct()) return;
  let d = null;
  text = (text || '').trim();
  if (!text) return toast('Paste your old save code first.');
  try { d = JSON.parse(text); } catch (e) { try { d = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(text)))); } catch (e2) {} }
  const inv = d && (Array.isArray(d.inventory) ? d.inventory : Array.isArray(d.cards) ? d.cards : null);
  if (!inv) return toast("That doesn't look like an old save code.");
  const tx = await dbRef('users/' + S.key + '/legacyUsed').transaction(cur => cur ? undefined : true).catch(() => null);
  if (!tx || !tx.committed || !tx.snapshot || tx.snapshot.val() !== true) return toast('This account already used its one-time import.');
  const MAX_CARDS = 400, MAX_COINS = 250000, perGod = {};
  let skipped = 0, capped = 0;
  const upd = {};
  let n = 0;
  for (const raw of inv) {
    const name = raw && raw.n;
    if (!CARDS[name]) { skipped++; continue; }
    if (n >= MAX_CARDS) { capped++; continue; }
    if (isGod(CARDS[name].r)) { perGod[name] = (perGod[name] || 0) + 1; if (perGod[name] > 2) { capped++; continue; } }
    const card = { n: name, at: Date.now() - 1000 + n };
    if (raw.trait === 'upside-down' || raw.t === 'upside-down') card.t = 'upside-down';
    upd['inv/' + newId() + n] = card; upd['dex/' + name] = 1; n++;
  }
  const oldCoins = Math.max(0, Math.min(Math.floor(Number(d.coins) || 0), MAX_COINS));
  const add = Math.max(0, oldCoins - (S.u.coins || 0));
  if (add) upd.coins = INC(add);
  applyLocal(upd); await writeUser(upd);
  try { localStorage.removeItem('cardio-save-' + S.key); } catch (e) {}
  closeModal(); renderProfile();
  toast(`Imported ${n} cards${add ? ' and ' + fmt(add) + ' coins' : ''}!${skipped + capped ? ` (${skipped + capped} skipped)` : ''}`, 'good');
}

/* ---------- AUCTION ---------- */
let auctionTime = 120, lots = [], botTimers = [], bidDrafts = {}, auctionTick = null;
const AUCTION_POOL = (() => {
  const by = { common: [], legendary: [], mythic: [], god: [] };
  Object.values(CARDS).forEach(c => { if (c.offsale || ['PURE GOLD', 'ANCIENT FOSSIL', 'PIRATE TOKEN'].includes(c.n)) return; (isGod(c.r) ? by.god : by[c.r]).push(c.n); });
  return by;
})();
const minRaise = bid => Math.max(10, Math.ceil(bid * 0.08));
const rand = (a, b) => a + Math.random() * (b - a);
function pickAuctionCard() {
  const r = Math.random();
  const pool = r < 0.32 ? AUCTION_POOL.common : r < 0.66 ? AUCTION_POOL.legendary : r < 0.92 ? AUCTION_POOL.mythic : AUCTION_POOL.god;
  return pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length)];
}
function startAuction() { newRound(); clearInterval(auctionTick); auctionTick = setInterval(tickAuction, 1000); }
function newRound() {
  botTimers.forEach(clearTimeout); botTimers = []; bidDrafts = {};
  lots = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
    const n = pickAuctionCard(), v = CARDS[n].v;
    const names = [...BOT_NAMES].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5).slice(0, 2 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 2));
    lots.push({ id: newId() + i, n, bid: Math.max(5, Math.floor(v * 0.5)), top: 'Market', mine: 0, wasMine: false, feed: 'Opening bid',
      bots: names.map(b => ({ n: b, max: Math.floor(v * rand(0.6, 1.45)) + 10 })) });
  }
  auctionTime = 120;
  if (curTab === 'auction') renderAuction();
}
function botBid(lot) {
  const need = lot.bid + minRaise(lot.bid);
  const able = lot.bots.filter(b => b.n !== lot.top && b.max >= need);
  if (!able.length) return false;
  const b = able[Math.floor(Math.random() * able.length)];
  const amt = Math.min(b.max, lot.bid + minRaise(lot.bid) * (1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3)));
  if (lot.top === 'YOU' && lot.mine) {
    const upd = { coins: INC(lot.mine), ['escrow/' + lot.id]: null };
    applyLocal(upd); writeUser(upd);
    toast(`Outbid on ${lot.n}! ${b.n} bid ${fmt(amt)}c. Your coins came back.`);
    lot.mine = 0;
  }
  lot.bid = amt; lot.top = b.n; lot.feed = `${b.n} bid ${fmt(amt)}c`;
  return true;
}
function placeBid(i) {
  if (!canAct()) return;
  const lot = lots[i];
  const amt = parseInt($('#bid-' + i).value, 10);
  const need = lot.bid + minRaise(lot.bid);
  if (lot.top === 'YOU') return toast("You're already winning this one.");
  if (!amt || amt < need) return toast(`Bid at least ${fmt(need)}c`);
  if ((S.u.coins || 0) < amt) return toast('Not enough coins!');
  const upd = { coins: INC(-amt), ['escrow/' + lot.id]: amt };
  applyLocal(upd); writeUser(upd);
  lot.bid = amt; lot.top = 'YOU'; lot.mine = amt; lot.wasMine = true; lot.feed = `You bid ${fmt(amt)}c`;
  delete bidDrafts[i];
  if (auctionTime < 10) auctionTime = 10;
  const round = lots;
  botTimers.push(setTimeout(() => {
    if (round !== lots || lot.top !== 'YOU') return;
    if (!botBid(lot)) lot.feed = 'Nobody has topped you... yet';
    if (curTab === 'auction') renderAuction();
  }, rand(900, 2600)));
  renderAuction();
}
function tickAuction() {
  if (!S.u) return;
  if (auctionTime > 0) {
    auctionTime--;
    let changed = false;
    lots.forEach(l => { if (l.top !== 'YOU' && Math.random() < 0.1 && botBid(l)) changed = true; });
    if (changed && curTab === 'auction') renderAuction();
  } else {
    botTimers.forEach(clearTimeout); botTimers = [];
    const upd = {};
    lots.forEach(l => {
      if (l.top === 'YOU') { upd['inv/' + newId()] = { n: l.n, at: Date.now() }; upd['escrow/' + l.id] = null; upd['dex/' + l.n] = 1; toast(`You won ${l.n} for ${fmt(l.bid)}c!`, 'good'); }
      else if (l.wasMine) toast(`${l.top} won ${l.n}.`);
    });
    if (Object.keys(upd).length && canAct()) { applyLocal(upd); writeUser(upd); }
    newRound();
  }
  const el = $('#a-timer');
  el.textContent = `${Math.floor(auctionTime / 60)}:${String(auctionTime % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  el.classList.toggle('hot', auctionTime <= 10);
}
function renderAuction() {
  $('#lots').innerHTML = lots.map((l, i) => {
    const need = l.bid + minRaise(l.bid), you = l.top === 'YOU';
    const draft = bidDrafts[i] && bidDrafts[i] >= need ? bidDrafts[i] : need;
    return `<div class="lot ${you ? 'win' : l.wasMine ? 'lost' : ''}">${cardHTML(l.n, { size: 'sm', attrs: `data-cn="${esc(l.n)}"` })}
      <div class="row"><span>Top bidder</span><span class="top ${you ? 'you' : ''}">${you ? 'YOU' : esc(l.top)}</span></div>
      <div class="row"><span>Current bid</span><b style="color:var(--gold)">${fmt(l.bid)}c</b></div>
      <div class="row"><span>Card value</span><span>${fmt(CARDS[l.n].v)}c</span></div>
      <div class="feed">${esc(l.feed)}</div>
      <div class="bid"><input class="field" type="number" id="bid-${i}" value="${draft}" min="${need}" data-draft="${i}" ${you ? 'disabled' : ''}><button class="btn pri" data-bid="${i}" ${you ? 'disabled' : ''}>Bid</button></div></div>`;
  }).join('');
}

/* ---------- WIRING ---------- */
function wire() {
  $('#login-go').onclick = doLogin;
  $('#login-name').addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') doLogin(); });
  $('#login-name').addEventListener('input', () => { pendingCreate = null; $('#login-go').textContent = 'Play'; loginMsg(''); });
  $('#tabs').onclick = e => { const b = e.target.closest('.tab'); if (b) showTab(b.dataset.tab); };
  $('#me-btn').onclick = () => showTab('profile');
  const setQty = e => { const b = e.target.closest('button[data-q]'); if (!b) return; qty = +b.dataset.q; $$('.qty button').forEach(x => x.classList.toggle('on', +x.dataset.q === qty)); updatePackAfford(); updateStageAgain(); };
  $('#qty').onclick = setQty; $('#qty2').onclick = setQty;
  $('#packs').onclick = e => { const p = e.target.closest('.pack'); if (p) openPack(p.dataset.pack); };
  $('#stage-again').onclick = () => lastPack && openPack(lastPack);
  $('#stage-done').onclick = () => { $('#stage').classList.add('hidden'); clearTimeout(revealTimer); };
  $('#stage-cards').onclick = e => { const c = e.target.closest('[data-n]'); if (c) cardModal(c.dataset.n); };
  $('#reveal').onclick = hideReveal;
  document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
    if (!$('#reveal').classList.contains('hidden') && (e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === 'Escape')) { e.preventDefault(); hideReveal(); return; }
    if ($('#stage').classList.contains('hidden') || $('#modal-root').innerHTML) return;
    if (e.key === ' ' && !/INPUT|TEXTAREA/.test(document.activeElement.tagName)) { e.preventDefault(); lastPack && openPack(lastPack); }
    if (e.key === 'Escape') $('#stage').classList.add('hidden');
  });
  $('#inv-q').oninput = renderInv; $('#inv-sort').onchange = renderInv;
  $('#inv-chips').onclick = e => { const c = e.target.closest('[data-f]'); if (c) { invFilter = c.dataset.f; renderInv(); } };
  $('#inv-grid').onclick = e => { const c = e.target.closest('[data-st]'); if (c) { const [n, t] = c.dataset.st.split('|'); cardModal(n, t, true); } };
  $$('[data-bulk]').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => bulkSell(b.dataset.bulk));
  $('#rank-chips').onclick = e => { const c = e.target.closest('[data-rk]'); if (c) { rankMode = c.dataset.rk; renderRank(); } };
  $('#rank-body').onclick = e => { const c = e.target.closest('[data-cn]'); if (c) cardModal(c.dataset.cn); };
  $('#lots').onclick = e => { const b = e.target.closest('[data-bid]'); if (b) placeBid(+b.dataset.bid); else { const c = e.target.closest('[data-cn]'); if (c) cardModal(c.dataset.cn); } };
  $('#lots').oninput = e => { const i = e.target.dataset.draft; if (i !== undefined) bidDrafts[i] = parseInt(e.target.value, 10) || 0; };
  window.addEventListener('beforeunload', () => { if (S.key && db) dbRef('online/' + S.key).remove(); });
}
function boot() {
  renderLoginFan(); wire();
  try { const last = localStorage.getItem('cardio-last'); if (last) $('#login-name').value = last; } catch (e) {}
  try { initDB(); } catch (e) { loginMsg("Can't reach the game server. Check your internet and refresh."); }
}

/* ================= LIVE TRADING ================= */
const Trade = (() => {
  const REQ_TTL = 10 * 60 * 1000, MAX_OFFER = 12;
  let me = null, online = {}, incoming = {}, out = null, room = null, popFor = null, lastChat = 0, pickQ = '';
  const myName = () => (S.u && S.u.name) || '';
  const fresh = r => r && r.at && Date.now() - r.at < REQ_TTL;

  function start(k) {
    me = k;
    dbRef('online').on('value', s => { online = s.val() || {}; if (curTab === 'trade' && !room) render(); });
    dbRef('requests/' + k).on('value', s => {
      incoming = {};
      Object.entries(s.val() || {}).forEach(([from, r]) => { if (fresh(r)) incoming[from] = r; else dbRef(`requests/${k}/${from}`).remove(); });
      $('#trade-badge').classList.toggle('hidden', !Object.keys(incoming).length);
      showPopup();
      if (curTab === 'trade' && !room) render();
    });
  }

  /* ----- requests ----- */
  async function send(name) {
    if (!canAct()) return;
    name = (name || '').trim();
    const k = nameKey(name);
    if (!name) return toast('Type the name of the player you want to trade with.');
    if (k === me) return toast("You can't trade with yourself.");
    if (room) return toast('Finish your current trade first.');
    let u;
    try { u = (await withTimeout(dbRef('users/' + k + '/name').once('value'), 8000)).val(); } catch (e) { return toast('Could not reach the server.'); }
    if (!u) return toast(`No player named ${name}.`);
    if (out) await cancelOut(true);
    const id = newId();
    await dbRef('rooms/' + id).set({ a: me, b: k, names: { [me]: myName(), [k]: u }, status: 'waiting', at: TS() });
    await dbRef(`requests/${k}/${me}`).set({ n: myName(), room: id, at: TS() });
    out = { id, to: k, toName: u };
    dbRef('rooms/' + id + '/status').on('value', s => {
      const st = s.val();
      if (!out || out.id !== id) return;
      if (st === 'open') { const o = out; out = null; dbRef('rooms/' + id + '/status').off(); enterRoom(id, o.to, o.toName); }
      else if (st === 'declined') { toast(`${u} said no to the trade.`); out = null; dbRef('rooms/' + id + '/status').off(); render(); }
    });
    render();
    toast(`Trade request sent to ${u}.`, 'cyan');
  }
  async function cancelOut(quiet) {
    if (!out) return;
    const o = out; out = null;
    dbRef('rooms/' + o.id + '/status').off();
    await Promise.all([dbRef('rooms/' + o.id + '/status').set('cancelled'), dbRef(`requests/${o.to}/${me}`).remove()]).catch(() => {});
    if (!quiet) render();
  }
  function showPopup() {
    const keys = Object.keys(incoming).sort((a, b) => incoming[b].at - incoming[a].at);
    const k = keys[0];
    if (!k) { popFor = null; $('#req-root').innerHTML = ''; return; }
    if (popFor === k + incoming[k].room) return;
    popFor = k + incoming[k].room;
    $('#req-root').innerHTML = `<div id="req-pop"><div class="muted" style="font-size:11px;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:.12em;">TRADE REQUEST</div>
      <div class="t"><b>${esc(incoming[k].n)}</b> has sent you a trade request</div>
      <div class="acts"><button class="btn green" data-acc="${esc(k)}">Accept</button><button class="btn" data-dec="${esc(k)}">Decline</button></div></div>`;
  }
  async function accept(from) {
    const r = incoming[from]; if (!r || !canAct()) return;
    if (room) { if (!(await askConfirm('Leave your current trade and join this one?', 'Switch'))) return; await leave(true); }
    if (out) await cancelOut(true);
    $('#req-root').innerHTML = ''; popFor = null;
    await dbRef(`requests/${me}/${from}`).remove();
    // Firebase runs the transaction first with null (nothing cached yet), so null must not abort it.
    const tx = await dbRef('rooms/' + r.room + '/status').transaction(st => st === null ? null : st === 'waiting' ? 'open' : undefined).catch(() => null);
    if (!tx || !tx.committed || !tx.snapshot || tx.snapshot.val() !== 'open') return toast('That trade request expired.');
    enterRoom(r.room, from, r.n);
  }
  async function decline(from) {
    const r = incoming[from]; if (!r) return;
    $('#req-root').innerHTML = ''; popFor = null;
    await Promise.all([dbRef('rooms/' + r.room + '/status').set('declined'), dbRef(`requests/${me}/${from}`).remove()]).catch(() => {});
  }

  /* ----- room ----- */
  function enterRoom(id, other, otherName) {
    room = { id, other, otherName, data: null, ready: false, done: false, seenMsgs: new Set() };
    const ref = dbRef('rooms/' + id);
    ref.onDisconnect().update({ status: 'left', by: me });
    dbRef(`rooms/${id}/here/${me}`).set(true);
    ref.on('value', s => onRoom(s.val()));
    dbRef(`rooms/${id}/chat`).on('child_added', s => addMsg(s.key, s.val()));
    showTab('trade');
    $('#req-root').innerHTML = '';
    sys(`${myName()} joined the trade`);
    toast(`Connected with ${otherName}!`, 'good');
  }
  function onRoom(d) {
    if (!room || !d) return;
    room.data = d;
    if (d.status === 'left' && d.by !== me && !room.done) { toast(`${room.otherName} left the trade.`); return exitRoom(); }
    if (d.status === 'cancelled') return exitRoom();
    if (d.status === 'done' && !room.done) { room.done = true; finished(d); return; }
    syncLocks();
    if (curTab === 'trade') renderRoomParts();
    maybeExecute();
  }
  function offerOf(k) { const o = (room && room.data && room.data.offers && room.data.offers[k]) || {}; return { cards: o.cards || {}, coins: Math.max(0, Math.floor(o.coins || 0)) }; }
  function sig() {
    const d = room.data, part = k => { const o = offerOf(k); return Object.keys(o.cards).sort().join(',') + ':' + o.coins; };
    return part(d.a) + '|' + part(d.b);
  }
  function offerValue(o) { return Object.values(o.cards).reduce((s, n) => s + (CARDS[n] ? CARDS[n].v : 0), 0) + o.coins; }
  function syncLocks() {
    locked.clear();
    if (room) Object.keys(offerOf(me).cards).forEach(id => locked.add(id));
  }
  async function writeOffer(o) {
    await dbRef('rooms/' + room.id).update({ ['offers/' + me]: o, ready: null });
  }
  function addCard(name, t) {
    if (!room) return;
    const o = offerOf(me);
    if (Object.keys(o.cards).length >= MAX_OFFER) return toast(`Max ${MAX_OFFER} cards per offer.`);
    const id = invList().find(c => c.n === name && c.t === t && !o.cards[c.id])?.id;
    if (!id) return;
    o.cards[id] = name; writeOffer(o);
  }
  function removeCard(id) { const o = offerOf(me); delete o.cards[id]; writeOffer(o); }
  function setCoins(v) {
    const o = offerOf(me);
    o.coins = Math.max(0, Math.min(Math.floor(Number(v) || 0), Math.floor(S.u.coins || 0)));
    writeOffer(o);
  }
  function toggleReady() {
    if (!room || !room.data) return;
    const d = room.data, mine = d.ready && d.ready[me];
    const o1 = offerOf(d.a), o2 = offerOf(d.b);
    if (!mine && !Object.keys(o1.cards).length && !Object.keys(o2.cards).length && !o1.coins && !o2.coins) return toast('Add something to the trade first.');
    dbRef(`rooms/${room.id}/ready/${me}`).set(mine ? null : sig());
    if (!mine) sys(`${myName()} locked in`);
  }
  async function maybeExecute() {
    const d = room && room.data;
    if (!d || d.status !== 'open' || d.a !== me || room.busy) return;
    const s = sig();
    if (!d.ready || d.ready[d.a] !== s || d.ready[d.b] !== s) return;
    room.busy = true;
    const rid = room.id;
    const tx = await dbRef(`rooms/${rid}/status`).transaction(st => st === null ? null : st === 'open' ? 'swapping' : undefined).catch(() => null);
    if (!tx || !tx.committed || !tx.snapshot || tx.snapshot.val() !== 'swapping') { if (room) room.busy = false; return; }
    try {
      const [ua, ub] = await Promise.all([dbRef('users/' + d.a).once('value'), dbRef('users/' + d.b).once('value')]).then(r => r.map(x => x.val() || {}));
      const oa = offerOf(d.a), ob = offerOf(d.b);
      const ok = (u, o) => Object.entries(o.cards).every(([id, n]) => u.inv && u.inv[id] && u.inv[id].n === n) && (u.coins || 0) >= o.coins;
      if (!ok(ua, oa) || !ok(ub, ob)) throw new Error('cards changed');
      const upd = {}, now = Date.now();
      const move = (from, to, fromU, o) => {
        Object.keys(o.cards).forEach((id, i) => {
          const c = fromU.inv[id];
          upd[`users/${from}/inv/${id}`] = null;
          upd[`users/${to}/inv/${id}`] = c.t ? { n: c.n, t: c.t, at: now + i } : { n: c.n, at: now + i };
          upd[`users/${to}/dex/${c.n}`] = 1;
        });
        if (o.coins) { upd[`users/${from}/coins`] = INC(-o.coins); upd[`users/${to}/coins`] = INC(o.coins); }
      };
      move(d.a, d.b, ua, oa); move(d.b, d.a, ub, ob);
      if (oa.coins && ob.coins) { upd[`users/${d.a}/coins`] = INC(ob.coins - oa.coins); upd[`users/${d.b}/coins`] = INC(oa.coins - ob.coins); }
      upd[`rooms/${rid}/status`] = 'done';
      upd[`rooms/${rid}/result`] = { [d.a]: oa, [d.b]: ob, at: now };
      await db.ref(ROOT).update(upd);
    } catch (e) {
      await dbRef('rooms/' + rid).update({ status: 'open', ready: null }).catch(() => {});
      sys('Trade did not go through because the cards changed. Lock in again.');
    }
    if (room) room.busy = false;
  }
  function finished(d) {
    const got = offerOf(room.other), gave = offerOf(me);
    locked.clear();
    const gotCards = Object.values(got.cards);
    const m = modal(`<h2 class="h" style="font-size:26px;">Trade complete!</h2><p class="muted" style="margin:6px 0 12px;">You traded with <b style="color:var(--text)">${esc(room.otherName)}</b>.</p>
      <div class="muted" style="font-size:12px;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:.1em;">YOU GOT${got.coins ? ' &middot; ' + fmt(got.coins) + ' COINS' : ''}</div>
      <div class="grid-cards">${gotCards.length ? gotCards.map(n => cardHTML(n, { size: 'sm', valueTop: true })).join('') : '<div class="muted" style="padding:10px;">No cards</div>'}</div>
      <div class="kv"><span>You gave</span><b>${fmt(offerValue(gave))}c of value</b></div><div class="kv"><span>You got</span><b style="color:var(--gold)">${fmt(offerValue(got))}c of value</b></div>
      <div class="cd-actions"><button class="btn pri" data-close>Nice</button></div>`);
    exitRoom(true);
  }
  async function leave(quiet) {
    if (!room) return;
    const id = room.id;
    if (!room.done) await dbRef('rooms/' + id).update({ status: 'left', by: me }).catch(() => {});
    exitRoom(quiet);
  }
  function exitRoom(quiet) {
    if (!room) return;
    const id = room.id;
    dbRef('rooms/' + id).off(); dbRef(`rooms/${id}/chat`).off();
    try { dbRef('rooms/' + id).onDisconnect().cancel(); } catch (e) {}
    room = null; locked.clear();
    if (curTab === 'trade') render();
    if (curTab === 'inv') renderInv();
  }
  function leaveAll() { if (room) leave(true); if (out) cancelOut(true); }

  /* ----- chat ----- */
  function sys(t) { if (room) dbRef(`rooms/${room.id}/chat`).push({ sys: true, t, at: TS() }); }
  function sendChat() {
    const inp = $('#chat-in'); if (!inp || !room) return;
    const t = cleanChat(inp.value);
    if (!t) return;
    if (Date.now() - lastChat < 700) return;
    lastChat = Date.now();
    dbRef(`rooms/${room.id}/chat`).push({ k: me, n: myName(), t, at: TS() });
    inp.value = '';
  }
  function addMsg(key, m) {
    if (!room || !m || room.seenMsgs.has(key)) return;
    room.seenMsgs.add(key);
    room.log = room.log || []; room.log.push(m);
    const log = $('#chat-log'); if (!log) return;
    log.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', msgHTML(m));
    log.scrollTop = log.scrollHeight;
  }
  const msgHTML = m => m.sys ? `<div class="msg sys">${esc(m.t)}</div>` : `<div class="msg ${m.k === me ? 'mine' : ''}"><span class="who">${esc(m.n)}</span>${esc(cleanChat(m.t))}</div>`;

  /* ----- render ----- */
  function render() {
    const root = $('#trade-root'); if (!root) return;
    if (room) { if (!$('#room')) renderRoom(); else renderRoomParts(); return; }
    const others = Object.entries(online).filter(([k]) => k !== me);
    const inc = Object.entries(incoming);
    root.innerHTML = `<div class="sec-head"><div><h2 class="h">Trading</h2><p>Send a request to any player by name. When they accept, you both join a live trade room with chat.</p></div></div>
      <div class="trade-start">
        <div class="panel">
          <h3 class="h" style="font-size:17px;margin-bottom:10px;">Send a trade request</h3>
          <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;"><input class="field" id="tr-name" placeholder="Player name" maxlength="16" style="flex:1;min-width:0" autocomplete="off"><button class="btn pri" id="tr-send">Send</button></div>
          ${out ? `<div class="waiting"><div class="spin"></div><div style="flex:1"><b>Waiting to connect...</b><div class="muted" style="font-size:13px;">Waiting for ${esc(out.toName)} to accept${online[out.to] ? '' : ". They're offline right now, so they'll see it when they log in."}</div></div><button class="btn sm" id="tr-cancel">Cancel</button></div>` : ''}
          ${inc.length ? `<h3 class="h" style="font-size:15px;margin:18px 0 8px;">Requests for you</h3><div class="online-list">${inc.map(([k, r]) => `<div class="orow2"><span class="avatar">${esc(r.n[0].toUpperCase())}</span>${esc(r.n)}<button class="btn sm green" data-acc="${esc(k)}">Accept</button><button class="btn sm" data-dec="${esc(k)}">Decline</button></div>`).join('')}</div>` : ''}
          <div class="notice" style="margin-top:16px;font-size:13px;"><div><b>Trade safe:</b> nothing moves until you <b>both</b> lock in. If anyone changes their offer, the locks reset.</div></div>
        </div>
        <div class="panel">
          <h3 class="h" style="font-size:17px;margin-bottom:10px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;"><span class="dot"></span> Online now <span class="muted" style="font-style:normal;font-size:13px;">${others.length}</span></h3>
          <div class="online-list">${others.length ? others.map(([k, v]) => `<div class="orow2"><span class="avatar">${esc((v.n || k)[0].toUpperCase())}</span>${esc(v.n || k)}<button class="btn sm" data-req="${esc(v.n || k)}">Trade</button></div>`).join('') : '<div class="muted" style="padding:8px;">Nobody else is online right now. You can still send a request by name.</div>'}</div>
        </div>
      </div>`;
  }
  function renderRoom() {
    $('#trade-root').innerHTML = `<div id="room">
      <div class="room-head"><span class="avatar" style="width:38px;height:38px;font-size:17px;">${esc(room.otherName[0].toUpperCase())}</span><div><h3>Trading with ${esc(room.otherName)}</h3><div class="muted" style="font-size:12.5px;" id="room-status">Connected</div></div>
        <button class="btn" id="tr-leave" style="margin-left:auto;">Leave trade</button></div>
      <div class="room"><div>
        <div class="offers"><div class="offer" id="of-me"></div><div class="offer" id="of-them"></div></div>
        <div class="fair" id="fair"></div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;"><button class="btn green big" id="tr-ready" style="flex:1;">Lock In</button></div>
        <div class="pick"><div class="pick-h"><h3 class="h" style="font-size:16px;">Add your cards</h3><input class="field" id="pick-q" placeholder="Search" style="margin-left:auto;width:160px;padding:7px 10px;" value="${esc(pickQ)}"></div><div class="grid-cards" id="pick-grid"></div></div>
      </div>
      <div class="chat"><div class="chat-h"><span class="dot"></span> Trade chat</div><div class="chat-log" id="chat-log">${(room.log || []).map(msgHTML).join('')}</div>
        <div class="chat-f"><input class="field" id="chat-in" placeholder="Say what you want..." maxlength="140" autocomplete="off"><button class="btn pri" id="chat-send">Send</button></div>
        <div class="chat-rule">Keep it about the trade. Never share your real name, school, address or phone number.</div></div>
      </div></div>`;
    const log = $('#chat-log'); log.scrollTop = log.scrollHeight;
    renderRoomParts();
  }
  function renderRoomParts() {
    if (!room || !$('#room')) return;
    const d = room.data || {};
    const mine = offerOf(me), theirs = offerOf(room.other), s = d.a ? sig() : '';
    const rMe = d.ready && d.ready[me] === s, rThem = d.ready && d.ready[room.other] === s;
    const side = (o, isMe, ready) => `<div class="offer-h"><div><b>${isMe ? 'You give' : esc(room.otherName) + ' gives'}</b>${ready ? ' <span class="ready-tag">LOCKED IN</span>' : ''}</div><div class="offer-val">${fmt(offerValue(o))}<small>c</small></div></div>
      <div class="offer-cards">${Object.entries(o.cards).map(([id, n]) => cardHTML(n, { size: 'sm', valueTop: true, attrs: isMe ? `data-rm="${esc(id)}" title="Tap to take back"` : `data-cn="${esc(n)}"` })).join('') || `<div class="muted" style="padding:30px 10px;text-align:center;font-size:13px;">${isMe ? 'Tap your cards below to add them' : 'Nothing yet'}</div>`}</div>
      <div class="offer-coins">${isMe ? `+ coins <input class="field" type="number" min="0" id="of-coins" value="${o.coins || ''}" placeholder="0">` : (o.coins ? `+ <b style="color:var(--gold)">${fmt(o.coins)} coins</b>` : '')}</div>`;
    const focused = document.activeElement && document.activeElement.id === 'of-coins';
    const ofMe = $('#of-me');
    if (!focused) ofMe.innerHTML = side(mine, true, rMe);
    else { const tmp = document.createElement('div'); tmp.innerHTML = side(mine, true, rMe); ['.offer-h', '.offer-cards'].forEach(q => ofMe.querySelector(q).replaceWith(tmp.querySelector(q))); }
    ofMe.classList.toggle('ready', !!rMe);
    $('#of-them').innerHTML = side(theirs, false, rThem); $('#of-them').classList.toggle('ready', !!rThem);
    const give = offerValue(mine), get = offerValue(theirs), tot = give + get || 1;
    $('#fair').innerHTML = `<div class="fair-bar"><i style="width:${give / tot * 100}%;background:var(--brand)"></i><i style="width:${get / tot * 100}%;background:var(--green)"></i></div>
      <div class="fair-l"><span>You give ${fmt(give)}c</span><span>${give === get ? 'Even trade' : get > give ? 'You win by ' + fmt(get - give) + 'c' : 'You lose ' + fmt(give - get) + 'c'}</span><span>You get ${fmt(get)}c</span></div>`;
    const btn = $('#tr-ready');
    btn.textContent = d.status === 'swapping' ? 'Trading...' : rMe ? (rThem ? 'Trading...' : 'Unlock') : 'Lock In';
    btn.className = 'btn big ' + (rMe ? '' : 'green'); btn.style.flex = '1';
    $('#room-status').textContent = rMe && rThem ? 'Both locked in. Swapping cards...' : rMe ? `Waiting for ${room.otherName} to lock in` : rThem ? `${room.otherName} locked in. Your turn.` : 'Build your offers, then both lock in';
    // pick grid (my inventory minus what's offered)
    const q = pickQ.toLowerCase();
    const avail = invList().filter(c => !mine.cards[c.id] && (!q || c.n.toLowerCase().includes(q)));
    const st = stacks(avail).sort((a, b) => CARDS[b.n].v - CARDS[a.n].v);
    $('#pick-grid').innerHTML = st.length ? st.map(s2 => cardHTML(s2.n, { size: 'xs', count: s2.ids.length, trait: s2.t, valueTop: true, attrs: `data-add="${esc(s2.n)}|${s2.t}"` })).join('') : '<div class="muted" style="padding:14px;">No cards to add.</div>';
  }

  /* ----- events ----- */
  document.addEventListener('click', e => {
    const t = e.target;
    const acc = t.closest('[data-acc]'); if (acc) return accept(acc.dataset.acc);
    const dec = t.closest('[data-dec]'); if (dec) return decline(dec.dataset.dec);
    if (!t.closest('#tab-trade')) return;
    const rq = t.closest('[data-req]'); if (rq) return send(rq.dataset.req);
    if (t.closest('#tr-send')) return send($('#tr-name').value);
    if (t.closest('#tr-cancel')) return cancelOut();
    if (t.closest('#tr-leave')) return leave();
    if (t.closest('#tr-ready')) return toggleReady();
    if (t.closest('#chat-send')) return sendChat();
    const rm = t.closest('[data-rm]'); if (rm) return removeCard(rm.dataset.rm);
    const add = t.closest('[data-add]'); if (add) { const [n, tr] = add.dataset.add.split('|'); return addCard(n, tr); }
    const cn = t.closest('[data-cn]'); if (cn) return cardModal(cn.dataset.cn);
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
    if (e.key !== 'Enter') return;
    if (e.target.id === 'chat-in') sendChat();
    if (e.target.id === 'tr-name') send(e.target.value);
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', e => { if (e.target.id === 'of-coins') setCoins(e.target.value); });
  document.addEventListener('input', e => { if (e.target.id === 'pick-q') { pickQ = e.target.value; renderRoomParts(); } });

  return { start, render, leaveAll, get room() { return room; } };
})();

boot();
</script>
</body>
</html>
